- **skill-updater**: Use this agent when you need to update the main SKILL.md file for the formspec-specs skill after reference maps have been created or updated. - **skill-updater**: Cultivates the ai-company Skills in two roles. ROLE 1 (Layer 1, monthly, human-triggered): aggregates diagnostic records across all projects, finds recurring failures, and opens a GitHub PR with concrete Skill fixes for a human to review and merge — never auto-pushes Layer 1. ROLE 2 (Layer 2, during a project): receives a worker agent's Layer-2 update proposal, runs an entrance check (format / duplication / contradiction), and writes it into the project's layer2.md. Invoke ROLE 1 only when a human asks to run the monthly cultivation; invoke ROLE 2 when a worker agent proposes a Layer-2 update. ([OyamaKumiko/claude-test](https://github.com/OyamaKumiko/claude-test/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-updater.md))

Úsalo cuando el usuario diga "aplica skins a &lt;juego&gt;", "añade skin &lt;x&gt; a &lt;juego&gt;", "diseña los skins de &lt;juego&gt;" o similar. ([aNd12326/Arcade-vault](https://github.com/aNd12326/Arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Aplica las skins clasico/neon/retro (con variante clara y oscura) a UN juego con motor real de Arcade Vault a la vez — el que el usuario indique explícitamente, nunca los cuatro en la misma corrida. Audita el motor indicado contra el contrato de skins, define o reutiliza las paletas, escribe el código de lib/games/skins.ts y del motor, y registra el avance en references/game-with-themes.md. Úsalo cuando el usuario pida "ponle skins a &lt;juego&gt;" o similar. ([bids-ekd/aracade-vault](https://github.com/bids-ekd/aracade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Audita e implementa los 3 skins obligatorios de todo juego con engine real en Arcade Vault: clásico (default), neon y retro, definidos en components/games/skins.ts. Verifica contraste ≥ 4.5:1 sobre el fondo negro del CRT y migra literales hex/rgba sueltos del engine a tokens del skin. Mantiene memoria en references/skin-audit.md. Úsalo cuando agregues un juego nuevo o sospeches que un juego existente rompe alguno de los 3 skins. ([carlosed7641/arcade-vault](https://github.com/carlosed7641/arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Recibe un juego ya implementado de Arcade Vault (`app/juego/&lt;slug&gt;/jugar/`) y garantiza que ofrezca al menos 3 skins seleccionables — Neon, Retro y Clásico, siendo Clásico el skin por defecto. Primero VALIDA contra la convención del repo (paleta por engine + selector UI) y, si falta algo, lo IMPLEMENTA extrayendo los colores del `engine.ts` a paletas, cableando la opción de tema y añadiendo el selector en `page.tsx`, sin alterar la jugabilidad. Verifica visualmente con capturas. NO crea juegos nuevos, NO toca el catálogo ni la base de datos. Úsalo cuando el usuario diga "revisa los skins de &lt;… ([dpperalta/arcade-vault](https://github.com/dpperalta/arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Adds the three canvas skins (Clásico, Neón, Retro) to one already-implemented Arcade Vault game. Refactors that game's hardcoded draw colors into a per-game palette module, wires the platform skin seam the first time it is needed, and records the palettes in .claude/skin-designer/palettes.md. Refuses and writes nothing if the named game has no engine. ([JamesPagani/arcade-vault-claude](https://github.com/JamesPagani/arcade-vault-claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Audita y, cuando hace falta, implementa un sistema de skins (paletas de color del canvas) para UN juego del catálogo a la vez, indicado explícitamente por el usuario. Garantiza al menos 3 skins por juego — clásico (default, preserva el aspecto actual), neon y retro — verificando contraste/legibilidad sobre el fondo oscuro propio de la estética arcade. Escribe el resultado en memory/skin_audit.md y en un TO-DO humano legible. ([livingstone23/claude-arcade_vault](https://github.com/livingstone23/claude-arcade_vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Aplica los 3 skins canónicos (clasico, retro, neon) al motor de un juego concreto de Arcade Vault, uno por invocación. No crea juegos nuevos ni toca Supabase. ([MarceloFernandez01/arcadia](https://github.com/MarceloFernandez01/arcadia/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-designer**: Revisa que un juego de Arcade Vault tenga al menos 3 skins visuales — neon, retro y clásico (default) — y reporta cuáles faltan. Si faltan skins, diseña sus paletas y entrega una propuesta lista para implementar. No implementa nada. Usar cuando se pida auditar o diseñar skins de un juego ("revisa las skins de snake", "diseña skins para tetris"). ([RobertoQuintero/arcade-vault](https://github.com/RobertoQuintero/arcade-vault/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-designer.md))
- **skin-reviewer**: Reviews hp-skin/1 manifests, sprite rectangles, and skin-rendering code against docs/skin-manifest.md and the decisions that govern the skin renderer (v0.4b onward). Read-only. Use on any change under skins/, design/, or the renderer. ([paperhurts/hurricane-party](https://github.com/paperhurts/hurricane-party/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skin-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **skincare-expert**: Dermatology and skincare specialist for business analysis sessions. Invoke when a design touches skin assessment, routine building, ingredient analysis, or concern-specific treatment (acne, aging, hyperpigmentation, sensitivity) — reviews claims against clinical evidence and cuts through marketing hype. ([Ameng2001/astra-studio-plugins](https://github.com/Ameng2001/astra-studio-plugins/blob/HEAD/studio-insight/agents/skincare-expert.md) · ★2)
- **skinner**: Use this agent after kirk has completed implementation. Skinner is a rigorous, read-only senior code reviewer that checks correctness, security, error handling, conventions, architecture, and testability against the ticket's acceptance criteria and the project's CLAUDE.md. Returns VERDICT: PASS or VERDICT: CHANGES REQUESTED. Invoke before leela in the pipeline. ([JoseMelecio/assistan](https://github.com/JoseMelecio/assistan/blob/HEAD/agents/skinner.md))
- **skip-analyst**: Analyzes whether a repeatedly failed track task can be safely skipped. Dispatched by the conductor:implement orchestrator when retry count is exhausted. ([AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor](https://github.com/AtaraxiaEpiphany/conductor/blob/HEAD/agents/skip-analyst.md))
- **skip-checker**: Fast classifier that decides whether a diff qualifies for an automatic SKIP verdict in /verify. Returns YES for doc-only edits, version-catalog bumps, lock-file regenerations, and pure generated-file changes; NO for anything that compiles or runs. ([juandgaines/greenfield](https://github.com/juandgaines/greenfield/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skip-checker.md))
- **skip-gate**: Spawned by the scout-router after the target is set. Decides whether scoring is worth it at all. Recommends skipping the sweep for very small repos or when the available signal is garbage (no git history, unreliable metrics), so the model is pointed directly instead. Writes scope.md. ([florianbuetow/claude-code](https://github.com/florianbuetow/claude-code/blob/HEAD/plugins/codebasescout/agents/skip-gate.md) · ★15)
- **skip-incoming-review-only-for-trusted-input**: Incoming task messages default to requiring user approval before the agent runs. From 28.1, `Agent Task Message Builder.SetRequiresReview(false)` starts the agent immediately. That is safe only for inputs you already validated in AL (your page action, your posting subscriber). External email or partner payloads are not trusted by default. Analysis Warnings still force a review. ([microsoft/BCQuality](https://github.com/microsoft/BCQuality/blob/HEAD/community/knowledge/agents/skip-incoming-review-only-for-trusted-input.md) · ★213)
- **skip-verifier**: Verifies whether a speculative skip (outline or detail) is safe based on session intent and outline artifacts. ([nirecom/agents](https://github.com/nirecom/agents/blob/HEAD/agents/skip-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **skipper**: The captain. Reads the project context (CLAUDE.md, diff, paths, user intent) and decides which specialist to invoke. Doesn't write code or docs — only routes. Coordinates the team based on the case. ([c-donnachie/skipper](https://github.com/c-donnachie/skipper/blob/HEAD/agents/skipper.md) · ★2)
- **skirnir**: The Allfather's assistant — the voice in and out of the house: the ear, the one mouth, the outbound gate, and the rooms he sits in. Headless: a Telegram turn, a desk notice, a door call from another system — never a window of his own. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/skirnir-assistant.md))
- **skirnir-assistant**: ../../.agents/agents/skirnir-assistant.md ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skirnir-assistant.md) · 5 copies)
- **skitarius**: Haiku mechanical gatekeeper. Runs the repo's actual validation gates (schema+integrity validate, targeted tests, regen-drift spot checks) after DSL data changes are applied, and parses failures into structured findings. No judgment — just gates. Use for "gate this change", "run the validators and report". Prompt lists which gates to run and the touched factions/files. Returns a single JSON object as final message. ([wn-mitch/40kdc-data](https://github.com/wn-mitch/40kdc-data/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/skitarius.md) · ★30)
- **skizzles-explorer**: Read-only investigation, reproduction, and implementation-path mapping. ([robertmsale/skizzles](https://github.com/robertmsale/skizzles/blob/HEAD/grok/agents/skizzles-explorer.md) · ★1)
- **skizzles-reviewer**: Independent adversarial review, verification, and acceptance assessment. ([robertmsale/skizzles](https://github.com/robertmsale/skizzles/blob/HEAD/grok/agents/skizzles-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **skizzles-root**: Skizzles root agent for direct implementation, bounded delegation, and final acceptance. ([robertmsale/skizzles](https://github.com/robertmsale/skizzles/blob/HEAD/grok/agents/skizzles-root.md) · ★1)
- **skizzles-worker**: Implementation ownership through focused validation and evidence. ([robertmsale/skizzles](https://github.com/robertmsale/skizzles/blob/HEAD/grok/agents/skizzles-worker.md) · ★1)
- **skorostnika**: **Мисия:** Мери реалните лабораторни метрики (FCP · LCP · TBT · CLS) в истински Chromium срещу локален билд и смята Lighthouse-съвместима оценка 0–100 по същите log-normal криви — ПРЕДИ деплой, когато поправката още е евтина. Връща конкретно какво пречи на 100, подредено по въздействие. ([stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few](https://github.com/stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few/blob/HEAD/agentgw/agents/skorostnika.md))
- **skorostnika**: Скоростника — измерен одит на производителността (Lighthouse-съвместим, в истински Chromium срещу локален билд), включително преди сайтът да е жив. Използвай преди деплой, при бавен сайт, регресия на Core Web Vitals (LCP/TBT/CLS) или нужда от сравнение преди/след; и когато някой каже „пусни PageSpeed преди да качим“, „защо не е 100“ или „сайтът е бавен“. Различен от SEO (откриваемост) и VPS-аджията (сървърна компресия и кеш). Лабораторните данни не са полеви. ([stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few](https://github.com/stefkostefko92-ux/Few-few/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skorostnika.md))
- **Skraft - Acceptance Designer**: Use when transforming refined stories and architecture decisions into executable BDD scenarios and implementation plans, before any code is written. Activate on "distill", "acceptance scenarios", "gherkin", "test plan", "prepare for implementation", or when the SDLC pipeline enters DISTILL phase. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/agents/acceptance-designer.agent.md) · ★8)

Dispatched after acceptance-designer produces DISTILL artefacts, or manually to audit existing Gherkin scenarios. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/agents/acceptance-designer-reviewer.agent.md) · ★8)
- **Skraft - Backlog Discoverer**: Use when discovering, triaging, or prioritizing GitHub issues for a project. Supports three discovery modes: user-assigned issues, artifact-driven discovery from code changes, and search-based exploration. Activate on 'discover backlog', 'triage issues', 'what should I work on', 'find open issues', or at the start of engineering work before invoking the skraft orchestrator. Runs standalone (product layer) — the developer invokes it directly; it is not part of the skraft engineering pipeline. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/agents/backlog-discoverer.agent.md) · ★8)

Dispatched after backlog-discoverer produces DISCOVER artefacts, or manually to audit a triage report. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/agents/backlog-discoverer-reviewer.agent.md) · ★8)
- **Skraft - Backlog Planner**: Use when refining raw GitHub issues into well-structured user stories with acceptance criteria, effort estimation, and milestone assignment. Activate on "refine", "plan sprint", "write stories", "split issue", "milestone planning", or when preparing a refined story before handing off to the skraft engineering orchestrator. Runs standalone (product layer) — the developer invokes it directly; it is not part of the skraft engineering pipeline. - **Skraft - Brownfield Analyst**: Use when the human chooses to analyze an existing/brownfield or legacy codebase that has no product docs or backlog — reverse-engineer it and produce an HVE-format PRD (docs/prds/) that HVE agents (GitHub Backlog Manager, ado-prd-to-wit, jira-prd-to-wit) can turn into issues and user stories. Activate on 'analyze this codebase', 'bootstrap a PRD', 'reverse-engineer a PRD', 'document this legacy system', 'produce a PRD from existing code', 'no docs, start from the code'. Activate on 'build a safety net for this service', 'characterize this API before refactoring', 'lock in current behavior', 'set up contract tests for this legacy service'. Standalone workflow — the human invokes it directly; it is not a Skraft - Orchestrator phase. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/agents/brownfield-harness-builder.agent.md) · ★8)
- **Skraft - Brownfield Refactorer**: Use when the human wants to refactor or replace part of an existing brownfield codebase that already has a safety net (characterization/contract tests) — recommend Mikado (in-place restructuring) or Strangler Fig (incremental replacement behind a facade), let the human choose, then drive the work leaf-by-leaf or slice-by-slice, keeping the safety net green at every commit. Activate on 'refactor this module safely', 'apply Mikado to this change', 'strangle this component', 'replace this service incrementally'. - **Skraft - Orchestrator**: Use when running the SKRAFT engineering pipeline from research to delivery (RESEARCH -&gt; DESIGN -&gt; DISTILL -&gt; DELIVER) — SKRAFT's equivalent of the HVE-RPI rpi-agent: an autonomous pipeline orchestrator that sequences phases, dispatches subagents, and persists resumable state. Consumes refined stories from the product layer; it does NOT do backlog discovery or story refinement (those are the standalone Skraft - Backlog Discoverer / Skraft - Backlog Planner agents, invoked directly by the developer). Automatically resumes from the last persisted state. - **Skraft - Software Engineer**: [Internal subagent — dispatched by Skraft - Orchestrator only] Delivers code via Outside-In TDD and Clean Architecture. Full PREPARE → RED → SYNTHESIZE-GREEN → COMMIT cycle with Object Calisthenics, mutation testing gates, and strict test integrity. ([SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin](https://github.com/SebastienDegodez/skraft-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/skraft-framework/agents/software-engineer.agent.md) · ★8)
- **Skraft - Software Engineer Reviewer**: [Internal subagent — dispatched by Skraft - Orchestrator only] Adversarial peer reviewer (Genesis A7): spawns 4 independent lenses, synthesizes a weighted verdict. - **Skraft - Solution Architect**: Use when designing software architecture for refined stories using Event Modeling, DDD strategic design (bounded contexts, context mapping), DDD tactical patterns (aggregates, value objects, domain events), producing Architecture Decision Records, component diagrams, and interface contracts. Activate on 'design', 'architect', 'ADR', 'event modeling', 'bounded context', 'aggregate', 'domain event', 'context map', or when the SDLC pipeline enters DESIGN phase. - **Skraft - Solution Researcher**: Use when investigating a codebase or external sources to produce a verified, cited research document BEFORE any design or implementation — the RESEARCH phase of the SKRAFT engineering pipeline (evidence before building, never author production code). Activate on 'research', 'investigate', 'find existing patterns', 'evidence before building', 'what does the codebase already do', 'spike', 'prior art', or when the pipeline enters RESEARCH. - **skraft-docs-derived-writer**: [Internal subagent — dispatched by skraft-docs-orchestrator only] Regenerates a `type: derived` handbook page from its plugin source (an agent, skill, lens, worker, gate, pattern or the citations data), in FR and EN mirrored. Copies the source's canonical vocabulary VERBATIM, follows the reference_template block contract, and keeps the FR/EN basename identical. - **skraft-docs-editorial-writer**: [Internal subagent — dispatched by skraft-docs-orchestrator only] Writes a complete `type: editorial` handbook page (vision, tutorial, explanation, narrative) in FR and EN, mirrored. - **Socrates**: Socratic tutor for students. Teaches by asking questions and never gives the answer or writes the deliverable.
